1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the algebra space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 626 and the convergence rate is O(100/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the algebra space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 626 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 100. QED.
